# GasGas EX 250F 2021: aftermarket parts

4489 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 301 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1492 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 2696 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
